- **jirahere-configure-get**: Drive `jirahere configure get` to read the five configured `defaults.*` values — `project`, `issue_type`, `current_quarter`, `previous_quarter`, `next_quarter` — straight from settings.json, as labeled text lines or one `--json` object. - **jirahere-plan**: Drive `jirahere plan` to resolve the current quarter's plan epic — the one work item whose title carries the `_Plan` marker — and render it as Markdown or JSON, in the same shape as `jirahere context get` at level 0, and `jirahere plan init` to create that epic when the quarter has none. `plan` mutates nothing; `plan init` is the one write and is idempotent. - **jirahere-plan-context**: Drive `jirahere plan context` to gather the facts needed to plan a day, a week or a month — the window's dates, the current quarter's active Vision, the plan epic, every quarter item with its age, each parent's most recently resolved Done child, and the circuit order with last and next position — as one Markdown or JSON bundle. - **jirahere-plan-day**: Default report layout for a day plan: seven parts in fixed order (header, circuit position, plan table, in progress, Vision line, flags, ask), fitting one screen. ([abrooke/jirahere-public](https://github.com/abrooke/jirahere-public/tree/HEAD/internal/skills/assets/jirahere-plan-day))
- **jirahere-plan-month**: Default report layout for a month plan: seven parts in fixed order (header, circuit coverage, work-stream progress, week-by-week themes, assets and cadence, structure, Vision drift). Triggered by "plan month", optionally with a start date. No command of its own; applies the shared rules in `jirahere-plan-synthesis` and offers to save with `jirahere plan save month`. Use when the user asks to plan a month; day and week layouts are in `jirahere-plan-day` and `jirahere-plan-week`. - **jirahere-plan-save**: Drive `jirahere plan save` to write an approved day, week or month plan into Jira as one work item titled `Plan &lt;Kind&gt; &lt;YYYYMMDD&gt;` under the current quarter's plan epic. - **jirahere-plan-synthesis**: Shared rules for synthesizing a day, week or month plan from the current quarter's Jira work: gather the facts with `jirahere plan context`, synthesize tasks, schedule, people and assets from the bundle, present the plan, and save it with `jirahere plan save` only after the user's go-ahead. No command of its own. Use when the user asks for a plan for a day, week or month, or asks "what should I do today"; the per-kind report layouts are in `jirahere-plan-day`, `jirahere-plan-week` and `jirahere-plan-month`. ([abrooke/jirahere-public](https://github.com/abrooke/jirahere-public/tree/HEAD/internal/skills/assets/jirahere-plan-synthesis))
- **jirahere-plan-week**: Default report layout for a week plan: seven parts in fixed order (header, last 7 days, day-by-day schedule, grouping, people, slipping, proposed changes). Triggered by "plan week", optionally with a start date. No command of its own; applies the shared rules in `jirahere-plan-synthesis` and offers to save with `jirahere plan save week`. Use when the user asks to plan a week; day and month layouts are in `jirahere-plan-day` and `jirahere-plan-month`. ([abrooke/jirahere-public](https://github.com/abrooke/jirahere-public/tree/HEAD/internal/skills/assets/jirahere-plan-week))

- **jirahere-rehome-children**: Reparent every direct child of one work item to another in a single bulk operation, with optional per-child summary substring and label swaps applied across each rehomed child's descendant tree, and optional status-based exclusion of children you want left alone. - **jisai-storybook**: 从故事、主题、人物/宠物照片、孩子涂鸦、已有角色或风格参考制作可逐页审校的完整绘本：封面、扉页、正文、封底，每页一张成品图，再合成整本 PDF 和翻页网页。正文支持代码排版或模型画进图里，用卡司定妆锚、风格锚、走位账、小样、单页重抽和断点续跑控制质量。用户请求“做绘本/图画书/storybook/picture book/把故事做成绘本/给孩子做一本书/出版级绘本/用照片或涂鸦当主角/做上一册的续集”，或修改已有绘本时使用。单张配图、只写故事、只翻译、纯印前文件处理不用本 Skill；漫画分格是实验能力，不保证自动达到出版验收标准。 ([giszzt/jisai-storybook](https://github.com/giszzt/jisai-storybook/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★4)

- **jiujiang-shuangzheng-product-material-organizer**: 为九江双蒸把客户提供的产品素材文件夹或 zip 压缩包整理入 Obsidian 产品素材库。用于“整理产品素材包”“导入九江双蒸 01.4_产品素材库”“按图片、视频、文档分类产品素材”“把图片分到产品实拍、好评截图、用户晒单、用户咨询”等任务；支持中文 zip 路径修复、素材盘点、按文件类型和产品生成预案、确认后复制入库和维护统一素材入库总表。不用于生成卖点卡、产品资料卡、朋友圈文案、合规审查、删除素材、覆盖原文件或低置信强制分类。 ([NewbieCheng/company-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/NewbieCheng/company-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/jiujiang-product-material-organizer/skills/jiujiang-shuangzheng-product-material-organizer))
- **jiujiang-shuangzheng-product-selling-point-card**: 用于分析、提炼和梳理具体产品的产品卖点，并生成或迭代“产品卖点分析报告”。当用户提出“分析这个产品的产品卖点”“提炼产品卖点”“梳理卖点”“这个产品该卖什么”“做产品卖点报告”等请求时应触发；产品名称可以是吉林双阳中段鲜鹿茸、黄精泡材等任意具体商品、原料或 SKU 名称，不要求包含“九江双蒸”。执行时只在九江双蒸项目已授权的产品卖点报告目录中识别同产品最新旧版：找到旧版就在唯一选中的基线报告上修正，没有旧版才首次分析；输出先打什么卖点、对谁说、凭什么说、哪些能说、缺什么证据以及执行后如何迭代。不用于重新做人群研究、直接写平台文案或完整销售话术、联网竞品研究、正式法律与广告合规审查、产品知识问答执行。 ([NewbieCheng/company-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/NewbieCheng/company-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/jiujiang-selling-point-card/skills/jiujiang-shuangzheng-product-selling-point-card))
- **jiujiang-shuangzheng-user-hypothesis**: 为九江双蒸的产品、规格、价格、渠道或发售任务建立和修正可验证的用户假设报告，以“人类决策层、证据与判断附录、AI结构化交接区”三层结构输出候选分群、优先用户、JTBD、购买机制、证据状态、验证计划及产品卖点卡下游接口。用于新产品寻找首批用户、重新判断优先人群、为产品卖点卡准备输入，或根据访谈、私域测试和发售反馈修正原假设；不用于直接写卖点、海报、销售信、完整发售方案、市场规模报告或扫描用户文档库。 ([NewbieCheng/company-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/NewbieCheng/company-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/jiujiang-user-hypothesis/skills/jiujiang-shuangzheng-user-hypothesis))
- **jiujiastudy**: 救驾（jiujiastudy）是留学生的 Canvas / Moodle 学习手帐：盯 deadline、排本周该学什么、按状态给建议。用户提到课程、作业、deadline、考试、Canvas、Moodle、老师，或说「现在什么情况」「最近要交什么」「这周学什么」「做完了」「没状态 / 累 / 来不及」「交之前检查」「想问老师」「做学习页」「一直在拖 / 开不了头」时使用；第一次用时自己完成设置，只向用户要学校网址（Canvas 另要 token）。 ([jiujiastudy/jiujiastudy](https://github.com/jiujiastudy/jiujiastudy/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★36)

- **jiuzhang-studio-architecture**: 九章工作室插件的整体结构与运行原理。涵盖目录布局、四层架构（DSH Host / SPA / 南风桥接 / ComfyUI）、REST API 全表、Agent 工具全表、数据目录结构、安装与依赖流程、以及"改哪里改什么"的定位表。当需要理解插件怎么跑起来的、要加功能、要排查"某个功能在哪实现"、或部署到新机器时使用。 ([yan-mc/ai-video-studio](https://github.com/yan-mc/ai-video-studio/tree/HEAD/skills/architecture))
- **jiuzhang-studio-setup**: 从零部署九章工作室插件（DSH 视频/图像创作插件）。当用户要安装、部署、修复或迁移这个插件时使用，涵盖环境准备、本地模型下载（H3 视频 + H3-Image 图像，约 68GB）、Blender 预演引擎安装、ComfyUI 配置、插件挂载与验收。插件本体不含模型权重、不含 Blender，必须由使用者自行下载。 ([yan-mc/ai-video-studio](https://github.com/yan-mc/ai-video-studio/tree/HEAD/skills/setup))

- **jive-joint-individual-variation-decomposition-scrna**: Use when multi-source or multi-batch scRNA-seq count matrices need batch-effect correction with preservation of cell-type signal, or when benchmarking JIVE against Seurat v5, Harmony, LIGER, and ComBat-seq under balanced versus unbalanced batch conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jive-joint-individual-variation-decomposition-scrna))
- **jive-multi-source-decomposition**: Use when you have two or more data matrices (e.g., scRNA-seq batches, or matched gene expression/methylation/miRNA sources like BRCA TCGA data) sharing samples/cells and need to separate shared signal from batch-specific variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jive-multi-source-decomposition))
- **jive-multi-source-rank-selection**: Use when you have multiple matched data matrices (e.g. BRCA gene expression, methylation, miRNA) and must decide the joint rank and per-source individual ranks before interpreting a JIVE decomposition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/jive-multi-source-rank-selection))
